## Part 6 — Code hygiene and maintainability: **7 / 10**

**Verdict.** The source tree is unusually clean for a hackathon-paced repo: no TODOs, no commented-out code, no hardcoded routes, no `process.env`/`console` leaks, no phantom dependencies, content fully schema-validated with zero dangling keys. What drags the score is documentation drift (architecture.md and domain.md still describe planned modules, jobs and functions that were built under different names or never built; testing.md/fr-audit counts are stale; CLAUDE.md says the guide is "a later version") plus real but moderate duplication (date formatting in 8 places, sheet scaffolding ×6, scattered retention/limit constants) and a handful of dead exports.

Everything below is from read-only inspection (`cat`/`grep`/`git`/python string parsing). Items that tooling would normally confirm are marked UNVERIFIED with grep evidence.

---

## Findings (most severe first)

### 1. HIGH — `docs/architecture.md` describes modules, functions and jobs that do not exist (or exist under other names)
- `docs/architecture.md:103-115` layout lists `jars/`, `limits/`, `reviews/` feature dirs. Actual: no `src/features/jars` (jars live in `src/features/ledger/components/jars.tsx`), no `limits` (it is `src/features/rules`), and the module is `review` not `reviews` (`find src/features -maxdepth 1`).
- `:126-156` dependency graph uses the same phantom names (`jars`, `limits`, `reviews`).
- `:162-167` ledger-writers table says `diary` writes via `record_entry`, `reverse_entry`. Migrations define `record_purchase`/`undo_purchase`; `grep -l "function (public\.)?record_entry" supabase/migrations/*.sql` → 0 files; `reverse_entry` → 0.
- `:562-571` scheduling table lists `schedule_reviews`, `generate_weekly_reviews`, `reconcile_and_retain` — 0 migrations define any of them; the three real `cron.schedule` calls are in `20261029130003_allowance_generation.sql:110`, `20261030164347_inbox_reminders.sql:93`, and the guide retention job at `20261030120000_guide.sql:406`, which the table omits.
- `:637` roadmap row names tables `spending_limits`, `notification_deliveries`, `mission_progress`, `weekly_reviews` and functions `create_child_account`, `family_now`, `evaluate_limits` — none exist (`create table` list in migrations has 30 tables: `money_rules` not `spending_limits`; no deliveries/weekly_reviews/mission_progress; `evaluate_spend_limits` not `evaluate_limits`).
- `:116` says brand components live in `components/ui/` incl. a `Money` component; they live in `src/components/brand` and `src/components/jar`, and `grep -rnE 'function Money\b|const Money\b' src` → nothing. `docs/design.md:347` repeats the `Money` claim.
- `:399` "The child's plan is deferred (FR02), so the estimate lands with it" and `:395` `goals.price_minor` — plan is built (`goals.plan_minor`, `src/features/goals/progress.ts`), column is `target_minor` (`docs/database.md:77`).
- `:59` proxy "attach a request id header": `src/proxy.ts` (34 lines) sets no header; `grep -nE 'request-id|requestId|x-request' src/proxy.ts src/lib/supabase/proxy.ts` → nothing.
- `:656` "swap is a single adapter in `notifications/server/channels`" — no such dir; adapters are `src/features/notifications/server/{email,push}.ts`.

**Why it matters:** this is "the map" new contributors (and the PR-review skill) are told to read first. Four of its names would send someone looking for code that was never written.
**Fix:** one docs PR: replace `jars/limits/reviews` with `ledger/rules/review`, rewrite §4.3 graph and the writers table from the real functions (`record_purchase`, `undo_purchase`, `post_request`, `confirm_handover`, `confirm_gift_handover`), replace §9 with the three real pg_cron jobs + `/api/cron/push`, drop `Money`, fix §5.2.

### 2. HIGH — `docs/domain.md` and `CLAUDE.md` still say the AI guide is a later version; domain.md's module table is mostly stale
- `CLAUDE.md:3` "…AI money guide in a later version". `docs/domain.md:5` "The AI guide (FR07, FR09) ships in a later version." Yet `src/features/guide` is 24 server files + 8 components, `docs/domain.md:62` marks `guide` ✅ in the same doc, and `REQUIREMENTS.md` "Later versions" says it was **moved into the pilot**.
- `docs/domain.md:48-62` "Planned feature modules": ticks only `goals`, `learning`, `guide`; `auth, profiles (partial)` row says "Add role, currency, nickname-only child onboarding" (all built); rows for `jars`, `limits`, `reviews` name modules that don't exist (see #1); `family`, `allowance`, `ledger`, `requests`, `diary`, `notifications` are built but unticked.
**Fix:** reword CLAUDE.md line 3 ("AI money guide behind `FEATURE_GUIDE`"), delete domain.md:5's sentence, and either tick every row or convert the table to "Feature modules" with real names.

### 3. MEDIUM — Date formatting re-implemented in eight places; the fr-audit follow-up says four and it has grown
`docs/fr-audit.md:64` lists "Date helpers are copied in four places; move them to `@/lib`" as a known follow-up. `grep -rn 'Intl.DateTimeFormat' src` (non-test) now shows the same "day numeric, month short, en-GB" formatter in:
`src/features/allowance/describe.ts:12-23` (`formatDay`), `src/features/guide/server/context.ts:98`, `src/features/guide/server/tools.ts:68`, `src/features/guide/components/parent-views.tsx:18`, `src/features/ledger/components/history-list.tsx:52`, `src/features/learning/text.ts:23` (`shortDate`), `src/features/requests/components/request-list.tsx:26`, `src/features/review/components/week-card.tsx:19`. `src/lib/dates.ts` has only ISO helpers (`familyToday`, `localDateOf`, `addDays`, `startOfWeek`). `allowance.formatDay` is used 21× inside its module and 0× outside — every other module rolled its own instead of promoting it.
Also `src/features/profiles/components/profile-card.tsx:12` uses `toLocaleDateString()` with no locale/timezone (hydration-mismatch risk if ever rendered; the component is currently unused, see #8).
**Why:** a child-facing app showing "2 Nov" one place and "02/11" another is confusing; timezone handling is subtle (describe.ts pins `timeZone: "UTC"`, others don't).
**Fix:** add `formatShortDate(iso, {locale, timeZone})` to `src/lib/dates.ts`, delete the seven copies.

### 4. MEDIUM — Six bottom sheets repeat the same ~45 lines of scaffolding
`put-in-sheet.tsx`, `take-out-sheet.tsx`, `ask-extra-sheet.tsx`, `goal-sheet.tsx`, `pause-sheet.tsx`, `purchase-sheet.tsx` (1,000 lines total) each contain, verbatim or near: `const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)` + `<Sheet…><SheetTrigger render={<Button variant="outline" size="lg" className="flex-1|w-full"/>}>` + `<SheetContent side="bottom" className="sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-lg"><SheetHeader>…` (e.g. `put-in-sheet.tsx:42-64`, `ask-extra-sheet.tsx:27-61`, `goal-sheet.tsx:42-59`, `pause-sheet.tsx:26-50`); the form idiom `const [idempotencyKey] = useState(newId); useActionState(async () => { const result = await runOnline(() => xAction(null, {...})); … return result }, idle); const fieldErrors = state && !state.ok ? state.error.fieldErrors : undefined;` (`put-in-sheet.tsx:89-99`, `take-out-sheet.tsx:120-129`, `ask-extra-sheet.tsx:74-86`); the identical success panel `<div className="grid gap-4 px-4 pb-4 sm:px-6 sm:pb-6"><p role="status" className="font-bold text-lg">…</p><Button … onClick={onDone}>Done</Button></div>` (`put-in-sheet.tsx:101-112`, `take-out-sheet.tsx:131-139`, `ask-extra-sheet.tsx:88-99`); and `<form … className="grid gap-6 px-4 pb-4 sm:px-6 sm:pb-6" noValidate>` + `<FormError error={fieldErrors ? undefined : state?.error} />` + `<Button type="submit" size="lg" className="w-full" loading={pending}>`. `take-out-sheet.tsx:47-57` and `ask-extra-sheet.tsx:29-37` duplicate the "refresh only when the sheet closes" `useRef` trick verbatim. Estimated duplicated scaffolding ≈ 250–300 lines.
**Fix:** a `BottomSheet({trigger, title, description, children})` wrapper in `src/components/ui` or `form`, a `SheetDone({message,onDone})` panel, and a `useOnlineAction(action, {onOk})` hook that returns `{state, action, pending, fieldErrors, formError}` (there is already `useOnlineActionState` in `src/features/offline/online-action.ts:107`, but the sheets bypass it and call `runOnline` by hand).

### 5. MEDIUM — Three spellings of the same ActionResult handling; `FormError` gating differs per form
All 17 `useActionState` users route through `FormError`/`FieldError` from `@/components/form` (good), but:
- Parent forms use `state?.ok === false ? state.error.fieldErrors : undefined` and `<FormError error={state?.ok === false && !fieldErrors ? state.error : undefined} />` (`gift-form.tsx:73,119`, `rename-child-form.tsx:51,72`, `set-pin-form.tsx:52,75`, `display-name-form.tsx:24,38`, `family-settings-form.tsx:37,75`, `allowance-form.tsx:57,137`, `parent-details-form.tsx:57,129`, `pause-sheet.tsx:77,112`).
- Child sheets use `state && !state.ok ? …` and `error={fieldErrors ? undefined : state?.error}` (`put-in-sheet.tsx:99,146`, `take-out-sheet.tsx:129,185`, `ask-extra-sheet.tsx:86,119`); `goal-sheet.tsx:204` has a third form `state && !state.ok ? state.error : undefined`.
- `remove-child-dialog.tsx:57` passes `state.error` even when `fieldErrors` exist (inconsistent, though harmless there).
- Three hook idioms coexist: `useOnlineActionState` (8 onboarding/auth forms), `useActionState + runOnline + useFormSubmit` (5 family settings forms), bare `useActionState + runOnline` (≈12 others). `purchase-sheet.tsx:69-121` defines its own `FormState` and bypasses `ActionResult` entirely (justified: it goes through the outbox).
**Fix:** derive `fieldErrors`/`formError` once inside the shared hook from #4; document in `docs/conventions.md` which idiom to use when.

### 6. MEDIUM — Child-facing retention and limit numbers are inlined across app copy, code and SQL
- **30-day chat retention** appears in 8 app places plus SQL: `src/app/(marketing)/privacy/page.tsx:63,97`, `src/app/(marketing)/safety/page.tsx:75`, `src/app/(app)/parent/children/[childId]/guide/page.tsx:24`, `src/features/guide/components/guide-switch.tsx:80`, `src/features/guide/server/service.ts:377` (`"Older than 30 days, so deleted."`), `:447` (`Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000`), and `supabase/migrations/20261030120000_guide.sql:370` / `20261031070000_guide_memories.sql:135` (`interval '30 days'`). **90-day memory** likewise: `privacy/page.tsx:63,97` and `guide_memories.sql:17,79,88`.
- **Guide limits** are spread over 9 files with no single config: `schemas.ts:5,8` (`MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH`, `MAX_TURNS`), `server/tool-specs.ts:19` (`MAX_TOOL_CALLS`), `server/provider.ts:70` (`MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS`), `server/pipeline.ts:63,147,150`, `server/claude.ts:33` and inline `max_tokens: 1024` (`:134,:238`) / `4096` (`:172`), `server/service.ts:37-38` (`PER_MINUTE`, `PER_DAY`), `server/tools.ts:49-53`, breaker threshold as a default parameter `server/breaker.ts:13`. `src/features/guide/server/config.ts` exists but holds only provider/budget getters. `TURN_TIMEOUT_MS = 52_000` with the identical 3-line comment is duplicated verbatim in `components/ask-jarso.tsx:19` and `components/guide-sheet.tsx:40`.
- **Nickname length 20**: `src/features/family/schemas.ts:14,84` + `maxLength={20}` in 5 components (`add-child-form.tsx:25`, `rename-child-form.tsx:66`, `child-sign-in-form.tsx:46`, `accept-invitation-form.tsx:56`, `start-onboarding-form.tsx:26`) + prose "Up to 20 letters" (`rename-child-form.tsx:69`). **140-char note**: `MAX_TEXT` defined separately in `requests/schemas.ts:4` and `allowance/schemas.ts:36`, repeated as `maxLength={140}` in 4 components.
**Why:** a privacy-notice number that disagrees with a migration is a trust problem for a children's app; today they agree only by discipline.
**Fix:** `src/features/guide/limits.ts` (client-safe) exporting the retention days and limits, imported by copy, service and the privacy/safety pages; a `NICKNAME_MAX` / `NOTE_MAX` in the schema files re-used by the inputs. The SQL intervals stay literal but get a comment pointing at the TS constant (and a pgTAP assertion if you want them locked together).

### 7. MEDIUM — `docs/api.md` contradicts itself; `docs/conventions.md` is behind the code in four places
- `docs/api.md:12` "There is no `/api/cron` route yet. … A cron route arrives with email delivery in the final demo." while `:58` documents `GET /api/cron/push`, and `src/app/api/cron/push/route.ts` exists.
- `docs/conventions.md:68` error codes list omits `UNAVAILABLE`, which `src/lib/errors.ts:9` defines and `src/components/form/field.tsx:66`, `online-action.ts:21,85`, `api.md:79` rely on.
- `docs/conventions.md:7` pre-commit = "Biome on staged files + typecheck"; `.husky/pre-commit` also runs `sh scripts/check-secrets.sh` first.
- `docs/conventions.md:28` hooks are `use-*.ts`: `useCurrentStep` is in `src/features/learning/components/current-step.tsx:26`; `useFormSubmit`/`useOnlineActionState` in `src/features/offline/online-action.ts:79,107`.
- `docs/conventions.md:30` "`…Action` suffix when it clashes with a service": practice is suffix-always (44 of 47 exports; only `signIn/signUp/signOut` in `auth/server/actions.ts` lack it). `:32` repository verbs `find…/insert…/update…ById/delete…`: actual repos use `call*` (13: `requests/server/repository.ts:153-288`, `goals/…:38-72`, `diary/…:10,39`), `fetch*` (5, e.g. `ledger/server/repository.ts:33 fetchBalance`, `requests/…:45 fetchBalances`, `notifications/…:103 fetchUnreadCount`) alongside 23 `find*`, plus `save*`, `set*`, `hit*`, `record*`, `confirm*`, `change*`. `fetch` vs `find` mean the same thing.
**Fix:** update the four doc lines to match practice (add `UNAVAILABLE`; "RPC wrappers are `call<Function>`"; "actions always end in `Action`"); rename the 5 `fetch*` to `find*` or document `fetch` = scalar read.

### 10. LOW — Very long components; `mission-player.tsx` is 824 lines
Heuristic scan (function start → matching brace at same indent, ≥80 lines): `MissionPlayer` `src/features/learning/components/mission-player.tsx:139-462` (324 lines) and `StepView` `:581-743` (163) in the same 824-line file; `AskJarso` `guide/components/ask-jarso.tsx:28-271` (244); `JarThread` `landing/components/jar-thread.tsx:73-308` (236); `GuideSheet` `guide/components/guide-sheet.tsx:54-277` (224); `HandoverForm` `allowance/components/handover-form.tsx:52-241` (190); `ParentRequestRow` `requests/components/parent-request-items.tsx:56-223` (168); `createAnthropicProvider` `guide/server/claude.ts:123-272` (150); `runGuidePipeline` `guide/server/pipeline.ts:173-306` (134). The server ones are linear pipelines with good section comments; the three UI ones (`MissionPlayer`, `AskJarso`, `GuideSheet`) each mix state machine, network and rendering. `AskJarso` and `GuideSheet` share the same send/reset/timeout logic (`ask-jarso.tsx:19,89` vs `guide-sheet.tsx:40,115`).

## Dangling content keys
**None found in non-test code.** 286 distinct keys/ids in `src/content/learning/**/*.json` (no duplicate JSON keys; all amount fields integer; 7 missions, 5 topics, 47 review items, 5 check-in questions). Content is parsed at **module load** (`src/features/learning/content.ts:33-36`, `server-only`), so an invalid edit throws on first import of `@/features/learning/server` — every learning page would 500 (no graceful fallback), but `content.test.ts` (12 cases incl. "keeps prompts to 25 words", voice rules, money tokens) gates CI. Whether `next build` itself executes it (ADR 0009:12 and content.ts:9-10 claim so) is UNVERIFIED without a run — with Cache Components it depends on whether the static shell render imports the module. Key literals in `src`/`evals` (`whats-in-spend`, `which-can-you-buy`, `whats-left`, `how-many-packs`, `snack-then-ticket`, `why-check-first`, `afford-or-not-yet`, topic keys) all resolve; the only non-matching literals are intentional fakes in tests (`not-a-step`, `not-an-item`, `late-1`, `how-many`, `split-r1` in `*.test.ts`). Reading level: 0 sentences over 25 words in content JSON or any `copy.ts`; no finance jargon hits (`ledger`, `idempoten…`, `reservation`, `budget…` → 0 in child-facing copy).

## Done well (evidence)
- **Zero** `TODO|FIXME|HACK|XXX` across src/tests/evals/supabase/.github (only a test string `"XXX"` currency); **zero** commented-out code lines; **zero** `console.*` in src; `process.env` read only in `src/lib/logger.ts:8,10` and `src/config/env.*` (rule 11 holds).
- **Zero** hardcoded route strings (`href="/…"`, `redirect("/…")`): all 94 route consumers go through `routes` in `src/config/site.ts:47-103`.
- File naming: 0 non-kebab files (only Next dynamic segments `[childId]`); every migration matches `^\d{14}_[a-z0-9_]+\.sql$`; 128 colocated `*.test.ts(x)`.
- Barrels are disciplined: every `server/index.ts` starts with `import "server-only"`; client barrels never re-export server services; `learning/index.ts:2-3` explicitly keeps content out of the client bundle.
- Error mapping is centralised: `src/lib/db-error.ts:4-16` is the only place Postgres SQLSTATEs (`JA401…JA503`, `22023`) are translated (one justified exception `goals/server/repository.ts:57` reading a `22023` message key); `FormError` already special-cases `UNAVAILABLE` calmly (`field.tsx:66-72`).
- `src/lib/*.ts` JSDoc: 22 of 33 exports documented, and the undocumented ones are self-describing types/overloads (`ActionResult`, `ErrorCode`, `moneySchema` overload signatures).
- Comment accuracy: a scan of every backticked identifier inside comments in `src` found **none** that no longer exists in the repo; numeric claims checked (5/min, 30/day, 45 s, 52 s, 60 s, 9 tools, 3 rounds, 6 look-ups, 40/20/60 learning caps) all match code/SQL.
- Hygiene of public repo: `.claude-logs/` committed (17 files), `.env*` ignored except `.env.example`, no build artefacts tracked (`tsconfig.tsbuildinfo`, `test-results`, `playwright-report` untracked).

## Part 6 — Code hygiene and maintainability: **7 / 10**

**Verdict.** The source tree is unusually clean for a hackathon-paced repo: no TODOs, no commented-out code, no hardcoded routes, no `process.env`/`console` leaks, no phantom dependencies, content fully schema-validated with zero dangling keys. What drags the score is documentation drift (architecture.md and domain.md still describe planned modules, jobs and functions that were built under different names or never built; testing.md/fr-audit counts are stale; CLAUDE.md says the guide is "a later version") plus real but moderate duplication (date formatting in 8 places, sheet scaffolding ×6, scattered retention/limit constants) and a handful of dead exports.

All from read-only inspection (`cat`/`grep`/`git`/python string parsing). Items tooling would normally confirm are marked UNVERIFIED with grep evidence.

---

## Findings (most severe first)

### 1. HIGH — `docs/architecture.md` describes modules, functions and jobs that do not exist (or exist under other names)
- `docs/architecture.md:103-115` layout lists `jars/`, `limits/`, `reviews/` feature dirs. Actual: no `src/features/jars` (jars live in `src/features/ledger/components/jars.tsx`), no `limits` (it is `src/features/rules`), module is `review` not `reviews` (`find src/features -maxdepth 1`).
- `:126-156` dependency graph uses the same phantom names.
- `:162-167` ledger-writers table says `diary` writes via `record_entry`, `reverse_entry`. Migrations define `record_purchase`/`undo_purchase`; `grep -l "function (public\.)?record_entry" supabase/migrations/*.sql` → 0 files; `reverse_entry` → 0.
- `:562-571` scheduling table lists `schedule_reviews`, `generate_weekly_reviews`, `reconcile_and_retain` — 0 migrations define them; the three real `cron.schedule` calls are `20261029130003_allowance_generation.sql:110`, `20261030164347_inbox_reminders.sql:93`, and the guide retention job `20261030120000_guide.sql:406`, which the table omits.
- `:637` roadmap row names tables `spending_limits`, `notification_deliveries`, `mission_progress`, `weekly_reviews` and functions `create_child_account`, `family_now`, `evaluate_limits` — none exist (30 `create table`s in migrations: `money_rules` not `spending_limits`; `evaluate_spend_limits` not `evaluate_limits`).
- `:116` says brand components live in `components/ui/` incl. a `Money` component; they live in `src/components/brand` and `src/components/jar`, and `grep -rnE 'function Money\b|const Money\b' src` → nothing. `docs/design.md:347` repeats the `Money` claim.
- `:399` "The child's plan is deferred (FR02)" and `:395` `goals.price_minor` — plan is built (`goals.plan_minor`, `src/features/goals/progress.ts`), column is `target_minor` (`docs/database.md:77`).
- `:59` proxy "attach a request id header": `src/proxy.ts:12-34` sets no header; `grep -nE 'request-id|requestId|x-request' src/proxy.ts src/lib/supabase/proxy.ts` → nothing.
- `:656` "single adapter in `notifications/server/channels`" — no such dir; adapters are `src/features/notifications/server/{email,push}.ts`.

**Why it matters:** this is "the map" contributors and the PR-review skill are told to read first; four of its names point at code that was never written.
**Fix:** one docs PR: `jars/limits/reviews` → `ledger/rules/review`; rewrite §4.3 and the writers table from real functions (`record_purchase`, `undo_purchase`, `post_request`, `confirm_handover`, `confirm_gift_handover`); replace §9 with the three real pg_cron jobs + `/api/cron/push`; drop `Money`; fix §5.2.

### 2. HIGH — `docs/domain.md` and `CLAUDE.md` still say the AI guide is a later version; domain.md's module table is mostly stale
- `CLAUDE.md:3` "…AI money guide in a later version". `docs/domain.md:5` "The AI guide (FR07, FR09) ships in a later version." Yet `src/features/guide` has 24 server files + 8 components, `docs/domain.md:62` marks `guide` ✅ in the same doc, and `REQUIREMENTS.md` "Later versions" says it was **moved into the pilot**.
- `docs/domain.md:48-62` "Planned feature modules": ticks only `goals`, `learning`, `guide`; `auth, profiles (partial)` row says "Add role, currency, nickname-only child onboarding" (all built); rows for `jars`, `limits`, `reviews` name non-existent modules; `family`, `allowance`, `ledger`, `requests`, `diary`, `notifications` are built but unticked.
**Fix:** reword CLAUDE.md line 3 ("AI money guide behind `FEATURE_GUIDE`"), delete domain.md:5's sentence, tick or rename every row.

### 3. MEDIUM — Date formatting re-implemented in eight places; fr-audit says four and it has grown
`docs/fr-audit.md:64` lists "Date helpers are copied in four places; move them to `@/lib`". `grep -rn 'Intl.DateTimeFormat' src` (non-test) now shows the same "day numeric, month short, en-GB" formatter in: `src/features/allowance/describe.ts:12-23` (`formatDay`), `src/features/guide/server/context.ts:98`, `src/features/guide/server/tools.ts:68`, `src/features/guide/components/parent-views.tsx:18`, `src/features/ledger/components/history-list.tsx:52`, `src/features/learning/text.ts:23` (`shortDate`), `src/features/requests/components/request-list.tsx:26`, `src/features/review/components/week-card.tsx:19`. `src/lib/dates.ts` has only ISO helpers (`familyToday`, `localDateOf`, `addDays`, `startOfWeek`). `allowance.formatDay` is used 21× in-module and 0× outside. Also `src/features/profiles/components/profile-card.tsx:12` uses `toLocaleDateString()` with no locale/timezone (hydration-mismatch risk; component currently unused, see #8).
**Why:** a child seeing "2 Nov" one place and "02/11" another is confusing; timezone handling differs (describe.ts pins `timeZone: "UTC"`, others don't).
**Fix:** add `formatShortDate(iso, {locale, timeZone})` to `src/lib/dates.ts`, delete the seven copies.

### 4. MEDIUM — Six bottom sheets repeat the same ~45 lines of scaffolding
`put-in-sheet.tsx`, `take-out-sheet.tsx`, `ask-extra-sheet.tsx`, `goal-sheet.tsx`, `pause-sheet.tsx`, `purchase-sheet.tsx` (1,000 lines total) each contain near-verbatim: `const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)` + `<Sheet…><SheetTrigger render={<Button variant="outline" size="lg" className="flex-1|w-full"/>}>` + `<SheetContent side="bottom" className="sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-lg"><SheetHeader>…` (`put-in-sheet.tsx:42-64`, `ask-extra-sheet.tsx:27-61`, `goal-sheet.tsx:42-59`, `pause-sheet.tsx:26-50`); the form idiom `const [idempotencyKey] = useState(newId); useActionState(async () => { const result = await runOnline(() => xAction(null, {...})); … return result }, idle); const fieldErrors = state && !state.ok ? state.error.fieldErrors : undefined;` (`put-in-sheet.tsx:89-99`, `take-out-sheet.tsx:120-129`, `ask-extra-sheet.tsx:74-86`); the identical success panel `<div className="grid gap-4 px-4 pb-4 sm:px-6 sm:pb-6"><p role="status" className="font-bold text-lg">…</p><Button … onClick={onDone}>Done</Button></div>` (`put-in-sheet.tsx:101-112`, `take-out-sheet.tsx:131-139`, `ask-extra-sheet.tsx:88-99`); and `<form … className="grid gap-6 px-4 pb-4 sm:px-6 sm:pb-6" noValidate>` + `<FormError error={fieldErrors ? undefined : state?.error} />` + submit button. `take-out-sheet.tsx:47-57` and `ask-extra-sheet.tsx:29-37` duplicate the "refresh only when the sheet closes" `useRef` trick verbatim. Estimated duplicated scaffolding ≈ 250–300 lines.
**Fix:** a `BottomSheet({trigger,title,description,children})` wrapper, a `SheetDone({message,onDone})` panel, and a `useOnlineAction(action,{onOk})` hook returning `{state, action, pending, fieldErrors, formError}` (there is already `useOnlineActionState` in `src/features/offline/online-action.ts:107`, but the sheets bypass it and call `runOnline` by hand).

### 5. MEDIUM — Three spellings of the same ActionResult handling; `FormError` gating differs per form
All 17 `useActionState` users route through `FormError`/`FieldError` from `@/components/form` (good), but:
- Parent forms: `state?.ok === false ? state.error.fieldErrors : undefined` and `<FormError error={state?.ok === false && !fieldErrors ? state.error : undefined} />` (`gift-form.tsx:73,119`, `rename-child-form.tsx:51,72`, `set-pin-form.tsx:52,75`, `display-name-form.tsx:24,38`, `family-settings-form.tsx:37,75`, `allowance-form.tsx:57,137`, `parent-details-form.tsx:57,129`, `pause-sheet.tsx:77,112`).
- Child sheets: `state && !state.ok ? …` and `error={fieldErrors ? undefined : state?.error}` (`put-in-sheet.tsx:99,146`, `take-out-sheet.tsx:129,185`, `ask-extra-sheet.tsx:86,119`); `goal-sheet.tsx:204` has a third form.
- `remove-child-dialog.tsx:57` passes `state.error` even with `fieldErrors`.
- Three hook idioms: `useOnlineActionState` (8 onboarding/auth forms), `useActionState + runOnline + useFormSubmit` (5 family settings forms), bare `useActionState + runOnline` (≈12 others). `purchase-sheet.tsx:69-121` defines its own `FormState` and bypasses `ActionResult` (justified: outbox path).
**Fix:** derive `fieldErrors`/`formError` inside the shared hook from #4; document in `docs/conventions.md` which idiom applies when.

### 6. MEDIUM — Child-facing retention and limit numbers inlined across app copy, code and SQL
- **30-day chat retention**: `src/app/(marketing)/privacy/page.tsx:63,97`, `src/app/(marketing)/safety/page.tsx:75`, `src/app/(app)/parent/children/[childId]/guide/page.tsx:24`, `src/features/guide/components/guide-switch.tsx:80`, `src/features/guide/server/service.ts:377` (`"Older than 30 days, so deleted."`), `:447` (`Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000`), and `supabase/migrations/20261030120000_guide.sql:370` / `20261031070000_guide_memories.sql:135` (`interval '30 days'`). **90-day memory**: `privacy/page.tsx:63,97` and `guide_memories.sql:17,79,88`.
- **Guide limits** spread over 9 files: `schemas.ts:5,8`, `server/tool-specs.ts:19`, `server/provider.ts:70`, `server/pipeline.ts:63,147,150`, `server/claude.ts:33` + inline `max_tokens: 1024` (`:134,:238`) / `4096` (`:172`), `server/service.ts:37-38`, `server/tools.ts:49-53`, breaker threshold as default param `server/breaker.ts:13`. `src/features/guide/server/config.ts` holds only provider/budget getters. `TURN_TIMEOUT_MS = 52_000` with its identical 3-line comment is duplicated in `components/ask-jarso.tsx:19` and `components/guide-sheet.tsx:40`.
- **Nickname length 20**: `src/features/family/schemas.ts:14,84` + `maxLength={20}` in 5 components (`add-child-form.tsx:25`, `rename-child-form.tsx:66`, `child-sign-in-form.tsx:46`, `accept-invitation-form.tsx:56`, `start-onboarding-form.tsx:26`) + prose `rename-child-form.tsx:69`. **140-char note**: `MAX_TEXT` defined separately in `requests/schemas.ts:4` and `allowance/schemas.ts:36`, repeated as `maxLength={140}` in 4 components.
**Why:** a privacy-notice number disagreeing with a migration is a trust problem for a children's app; today they agree only by discipline.
**Fix:** `src/features/guide/limits.ts` (client-safe) exporting retention days and limits for copy, service and the privacy/safety pages; `NICKNAME_MAX`/`NOTE_MAX` constants reused by inputs; SQL intervals commented to point at the TS constant (pgTAP assertion optional).

### 7. MEDIUM — `docs/api.md` contradicts itself; `docs/conventions.md` is behind the code in four places
- `docs/api.md:12` "There is no `/api/cron` route yet…" while `:58` documents `GET /api/cron/push` and `src/app/api/cron/push/route.ts` exists.
- `docs/conventions.md:68` error codes list omits `UNAVAILABLE` (`src/lib/errors.ts:9`; relied on by `field.tsx:66`, `online-action.ts:21,85`, `api.md:79`).
- `docs/conventions.md:7` pre-commit = "Biome + typecheck"; `.husky/pre-commit` also runs `sh scripts/check-secrets.sh`.
- `docs/conventions.md:28` hooks `use-*.ts`: `useCurrentStep` is in `learning/components/current-step.tsx:26`; `useFormSubmit`/`useOnlineActionState` in `offline/online-action.ts:79,107`.
- `docs/conventions.md:30` "`…Action` suffix when it clashes": practice is suffix-always (44/47; only `signIn/signUp/signOut`). `:32` repo verbs `find/insert/update/delete`: actual repos use `call*` (13: `requests/server/repository.ts:153-288`, `goals/…:38-72`, `diary/…:10,39`), `fetch*` (5: `ledger/…:33 fetchBalance`, `requests/…:45,69`, `notifications/…:103`, `allowance/…:81`) alongside 23 `find*`, plus `save*`, `set*`, `hit*`, `record*`, `confirm*`, `change*`. `fetch` vs `find` mean the same thing.
**Fix:** update the four doc lines (add `UNAVAILABLE`; "RPC wrappers are `call<Function>`"; "actions always end in `Action`"); rename the 5 `fetch*` to `find*` or document `fetch` = scalar read.

### 8. MEDIUM (UNVERIFIED, grep-evidenced) — Dead or unreferenced exports
Scan: every `export` in non-test, non-barrel files counted across all of `src` (declaration excluded).
- `JarsoWordmark` (`src/components/brand/jarso-wordmark.tsx`) — 0 references except the brand barrel; `grep -rn wordmark src` → no consumer. `docs/design.md:15` claims the app serves wordmarks through it.
- `ProfileCard` (`src/features/profiles/components/profile-card.tsx`) — 0 references; barrel-exported `profiles/index.ts:5`.
- `reviewItemsFor`, `topicForSkill` (`src/features/learning/content.ts:49-55`) — 0 references incl. tests.
- `wordCount` (`learning/text.ts`) — test-only (4). `MissionAttempt` type (`learning/types.ts`) — 0.
- Eleven inferred `*Input`/`*Params` types never read: `AddChildInput`, `InviteParams` (family/schemas.ts), `AllowanceInput`, `ChangeAllowanceInput`, `GiftInput`, `HandoverInput`, `PauseInput`, `UpdateGiftInput` (allowance/schemas.ts), `SignInInput`, `SignUpInput` (auth/schemas.ts), `HistoryParams` (ledger/schemas.ts, re-exported `ledger/index.ts:7`).
- Exported with no consumer outside own file: `depositMessage` (`put-in-sheet.tsx:68`), `withdrawalMessage` (`take-out-sheet.tsx`), `emergencyMessage` (`ask-extra-sheet.tsx:64`), `previewLine` (`goal-sheet.tsx:62`), `isPushConfigured` (barrel `notifications/server/index.ts:5`, used only `push.ts:77,146`), `errorCodes` (`lib/errors.ts:1`), plus ~20 row/helper types (`AttemptRow`, `GiftRow`, `ScheduleRow`, `LatestSplit`, `ClosedOccurrence`, `OccurrenceAmounts`, `PinCheck`, `Balances`, …).

# Part 7 — Performance and resilience (read-only audit)

**Score: 8 / 10.** The app is built the way the production-readiness skill asks for at this scale: every Supabase call has an 8 s deadline, session and family context are React-`cache()`d so `getClaims()` runs once per request with no GoTrue round trip, pages use `Promise.all` and per-section `Suspense` with fail-soft islands, no `"use cache"` exists so nothing can leak between users, and the guide has deadlines, DB-backed limits and idempotent re-sends. The points lost are one real correctness gap in the outbox retry budget, a seven-RPC balance fan-out that multiplies across children, and push delivery for cron-generated notices that waits up to a day.

All findings are from reading source only; nothing was run. Items that would need a run/profile to confirm are marked UNVERIFIED.

---

## Findings (most severe first)

### 1. Medium — Outbox queued purchases expire after ~17 minutes of server errors, not 7 days
- `src/features/offline/outbox/state.ts:6-9` — `BACKOFF_MS = [2_000, 5_000, 15_000, 30_000]`, `BACKOFF_CAP_MS = 60_000`, `MAX_ATTEMPTS = 20`, `MAX_AGE_MS = 7 days`.
- `state.ts:29-36` — on `retry`, `attempts >= MAX_ATTEMPTS || tooOld` → purchase becomes `needs_look` with `offlineCopy.expired`.
- `src/features/offline/outbox/send.ts:52-55` — any `>= 500` is `retry` (and `src/lib/timed-fetch.ts:13-18` turns a slow/unreachable Supabase into a 503 JA503, so a Supabase blip is a 5xx here).
- `src/features/offline/components/outbox-sync.tsx:9, 32-36, 39-48` — a 30 s poll plus a one-shot timer at `nextAttemptAt` make every attempt fire as soon as it is due.

Arithmetic: 2+5+15+30 s, then 16 × 60 s ≈ 17 min to burn 20 attempts. A 20-minute Supabase outage, a deploy with a broken route, or a captive-portal Wi-Fi where `navigator.onLine` stays `true` (flush's `offline()` check at `flush.ts:34-36, 45` only trusts the browser flag) pushes every queued purchase into "needs a look", and the child has to re-enter it. The 7-day `MAX_AGE_MS` is effectively unreachable on this path. For a children's money diary, silently giving up on a saved purchase after a short outage is the wrong failure mode.

Fix (right-sized): make age the only expiry and let attempts drive backoff only — e.g. drop the `attempts >= MAX_ATTEMPTS` branch, or do not increment `attempts` for `>= 500`/network outcomes (only for 408/timeouts), and raise `BACKOFF_CAP_MS` to 5–10 minutes so a long outage does not hammer the route.

### 2. Medium — Balances are 7 round trips per child; 10 aggregate scans for 4 numbers; ×N on parent pages
- `src/features/requests/server/repository.ts:45-54` — `fetchBalances` issues six RPCs: `balance(spend)`, `balance(save)`, `reserved_save`, `held_spend`, `available_save`, `available_spend`; `repository.ts:69-73` adds `withdrawal_cap_remaining` (`service.ts:52-55`) → 7 PostgREST HTTP calls per `getAvailability`.
- `supabase/migrations/20261031020000_requests.sql:103-131` — `available_save` re-runs `balance(...) - reserved_save(...)` and `available_spend` re-runs `balance(...) - held_spend(...)`, so the database does 10 aggregate scans to return 4 distinct values.
- `src/app/(app)/parent/page.tsx:58-66` — per child the page starts `listDue`, `getAvailability`, `getGoalView`, `listRequestsNeedingParent`, `getSchedule`. Per child that is: `is_parent_of` (1, cached per child via `dal.ts:80-82`) + `list_due_for_parent` (1) + balances (6) + cap (1) + `goal_progress` + `goals` (2) + requests (1) + schedule + `allowance_next_due_on` (2) = **14**. Family with 3 children ≈ 42 per-child queries + membership, family (×2, see #6), children, profile-completion gate, unread count, limit notices ≈ **~50 round trips per parent-home render** (and again on every tab focus, see #8).
- `src/features/ledger/server/service.ts:27-35` — `getJars` is a second pair of `balance` RPCs on the parent child page.

They are started concurrently (good, latency is not N+1-shaped), but each is a separate HTTPS call to PostgREST from a serverless function, which is the cost that actually shows up as tail latency at pilot scale (UNVERIFIED without a trace).

Fix: one `security definer` function `child_balances(p_child_id uuid)` returning a single row `(spend_minor, save_minor, reserved_save_minor, held_spend_minor, available_save_minor, available_spend_minor, cap_remaining_minor)` built from two CTE aggregates (ledger_entries grouped by jar; requests grouped by kind/status) plus the existing `withdrawal_cap_remaining` logic. `fetchBalances` + `fetchCapRemaining` become one `rpc`, and `getJars` can read the same row. 7 → 1 per child; parent home ≈ 50 → ~30.

### 3. Medium — Push pings for cron-generated notices wait for the daily sweep; a 429 loses the ping for good
- `vercel.json:4` — `"schedule": "30 1 * * *"` (daily).
- `src/features/notifications/server/push.ts:71-74, 145-154` — push runs `after()` an action, or from the daily cron. Nothing fires it for notices that pg_cron writes.
- `supabase/migrations/20261030164347_inbox_reminders.sql:93-96` — `send-handover-reminders` runs `'7 * * * *'` (hourly) and inserts notices with no push trigger, so a reminder written at 15:07 is pushed at 01:30 UTC the next day (10:30 JST for `regions: ["hnd1"]`, `vercel.json:3`). The in-app realtime path still works, but the point of FR16 is the ping when Jarso is closed.
- `push.ts:106-115` — a 429 (or any non-404/410 error) increments `failed` only; the notice was already claimed (`pushed_at = now()` in `20261031080000_push_subscriptions.sql:125-128`) so it is never retried.

Fix: keep the daily sweep as the safety net, and add an hourly trigger from the database side — `pg_cron` + `pg_net` POSTing `/api/cron/push` with `CRON_SECRET` from Vault right after `send_handover_reminders` — or, if on Vercel Pro, change the cron to hourly. For 429: in the catch, when `status === 429` run a single `update notifications set pushed_at = null where id = ...` so the next sweep retries it.

### 4. Low — Parent chat list loads every message of up to 50 conversations to show a one-line preview
- `src/features/guide/server/service.ts:415-429` — `listGuideChats` calls `listMessages(db, conversations.map(c => c.id))` and then filters in JS; `summarize` (`service.ts:397-412`) uses only the first child message's text.
- `src/features/guide/server/repository.ts:104-119, 150-173` — 50 conversations × up to 2×`MAX_TURNS` (= 12, `src/features/guide/schemas.ts:8`) rows with full `text` → up to ~1,200 rows of transcript per parent page view.

Fix: store `preview` on `guide_conversations` when the first turn is recorded (`guide_record_turn`), or fetch `distinct on (conversation_id)` first child messages in a small RPC. Keeps `listMessages` for the transcript page only.

### 5. Low — Guide rate-limit counters are consumed before the turn runs, so outages eat the child's daily allowance
- `src/features/guide/server/service.ts:213-218` — `hit_rate_limit` for minute and day is called in `prepareGuideTurn`; `runGuideTurn` (`service.ts:254-257`) may then answer with fixed "unavailable" copy because the breaker is open or budget is spent. Each such turn still costs one of the 30/day. Acceptable at pilot, but worth a line in the PR: check `guideBreaker.isOpen()`/`budgetSpent()` before hitting the day counter, or do not count `route === "unavailable"` turns.

### 6. Low — `getFamily()` is not memoised, so the families row is read 2–3× per request
- `src/features/family/server/service.ts:84-88` — plain `async function`, while `getMembership` (`dal.ts:17`) and friends are `cache()`d.
- Call sites on one child-home request: `getFamilyContext` from `src/app/(app)/child/page.tsx:31` and `getFamily` from `src/app/(app)/child/layout.tsx:66`; diary adds `getHistoryPage` → `getFamily` (`ledger/service.ts:148`); parent home: `page.tsx:52` plus `parent/layout.tsx:45`.
Fix: `export const getFamily = cache(async () => …)`. One line, saves one round trip on nearly every page.

### 7. Low — Guide tool look-ups do not share the turn's abort signal
- `src/features/guide/server/claude.ts:205-215` — `tools.run(call.name, call.input)` is awaited with no `signal`; `src/features/guide/server/tools.ts` has no timeout of its own (grep for `timeout|AbortSignal|Promise.race` returns nothing). The Supabase client's 8 s fetch deadline (`src/lib/timed-fetch.ts:87`) bounds each query, so the worst case is a few extra seconds inside the 45 s turn deadline (`pipeline.ts:147`). Note only; fine as is.

### 8. Low (Know) — Every tab focus and every realtime notice re-renders the whole page tree
- `src/components/shell/inbox-refresh.tsx:29-35` — `router.refresh()` on every `visibilitychange` to visible.
- `src/features/notifications/components/notice-listener.tsx:26-32, 43-46` — `router.refresh()` 300 ms after any notice insert/update and on each (re)join.
Each refresh re-runs the full RSC tree (≈50 queries on a 3-child parent home, see #2). At 60 users this is harmless; it becomes the first thing to revisit if Supabase query volume ever matters. The #2 fix shrinks it the most.

### 9. Low — Two small index-shape mismatches on history reads
- `supabase/migrations/20261029183422_purchases.sql:340-345` — `list_ledger_history` filters `child_id, mode` and sorts `occurred_at desc, created_at desc`, but the index is `(child_id, mode, jar, occurred_at desc)` (`20261029130001_ledger.sql:66-67`): `jar` sits between, so the planner uses the two-column prefix and sorts. A per-child ledger is small; UNVERIFIED impact. A `(child_id, mode, occurred_at desc)` index would make it an ordered index scan if `explain` ever shows a sort.
- `src/features/ledger/server/repository.ts:92-99` — `listClosedOccurrences` filters `status='closed'` and orders by `confirmed_at`; only `(child_id, due_on …)` indexes exist (`20261029130002_allowance_schedules.sql:79-83`). Same caveat.
- `src/features/ledger/server/service.ts:154-157` — `listOccurrenceAmounts` is a dependent second round trip after the rows arrive; it could be a join inside `list_ledger_history`. Nit.

### 10. Low — `revalidatePath` used in two action files where everything else uses `refresh()`
- `src/features/goals/server/actions.ts:3, 14, 21` and `src/features/notifications/server/actions.ts:3, 12-13`. With `cacheComponents: true` (`next.config.ts:12`) and no `"use cache"`, these per-user pages are not cached, so `revalidatePath` is effectively a no-op; correctness is rescued because the client components call `router.refresh()` (`goal-sheet.tsx:104, 223`, `use-open-notice.ts:21`). Switch to `refresh()` for consistency with `allowance/requests/rules/diary/profiles` actions.

### 11. Info — Outbox on a shared device / sign-out
- `src/features/offline/outbox/store.ts:48-52` keys reads by `userId`; `src/app/api/v1/outbox/route.ts:63-65` rejects a body whose `userId` differs from the session (401), and `send.ts:56` + `state.ts:24-26` keep the item untouched for when that child is back. So child A's queued purchase can never post under child B. The store is not cleared on sign-out, so a queued purchase (label + amount) stays in IndexedDB until A signs in again; there is no cap on entry count (`submit.ts:54-57`). Acceptable for a pilot; a `clear(userId)` on sign-out would be a nice-to-have.

### 12. Info — Pricing constants vs the model table
- `src/features/guide/server/cost.ts:8-13` — Sonnet 5.5 `{2, 10, 0.2}`, Opus 5.5 `{4, 20, 0.2}`, Haiku 5.5 `{0.1, 0.5, …}` match the Anthropic table I have (Sonnet 5.5 $2/$10, cache read $0.20; Opus 5.5 $4/$20, cache read $0.20; Haiku 5.5 $0.10/$0.50 up to 100K prompt). Cache write at 1.25× input (`cost.ts:27`) is the 5-minute-TTL rate, which is what `cache_control: {type: "ephemeral"}` (`claude.ts:176, 180`) uses. Haiku cache-read `0.01` is UNVERIFIED but never exercised: the screens pass only `screenInput`/`screenOutput` (`claude.ts:148, 154, 268`). Cost is a float in cents (`cost.ts:33`) computed from integer token sums and used only for the budget comparison; the stored record is tokens, not money (`repository.ts:255-262`), so no rounding accumulates.

## A. Page waterfall table

Session: `getCurrentUser` is `cache()`d (`src/features/auth/server/session.ts:15`) and uses `supabase.auth.getClaims()` (`:20`), i.e. local JWT verification, not a `getUser()` round trip to GoTrue; `getMembership`, `getRole(userId)`, `parentOfChild(childId)` are also `cache()`d (`src/features/family/server/dal.ts:17, 24, 80`). So every page tree below does **one** claims check and **one** membership query per request however many components call `requireRole`/`assertRole`/`getCurrentUser`.

| Page | Independent sequential awaits (could be `Promise.all`) | `Promise.all`? | Suspense boundaries | Session fetches / request |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `(app)/layout.tsx` | 0 | — | 2 (`InboxRefresh`, `ProfileGate`), `fallback={null}` | 1 (cached; `profile-gate.tsx:20-22` also 1 membership + 1 `parent_details`) |
| `child/layout.tsx` | 0 (`ChildNoticeListener` does `getCurrentUser → currentRole → getFamily` in series, `:63-66`, but all cached except `getFamily`) | — | 4 islands; nav shows without count on slow/failed read | 1 (cached) |
| `parent/layout.tsx` | 0 | — | 3 islands | 1 (cached) |
| `child/page.tsx` | 0 (`requireRole` must precede, then `Promise.all` of 4 `:29-34`) | Yes ×5 (`:29, 90, 137, 207`) | 6 (outer + Jarso, Step, Shelf, Week, Skills); jars failing never takes Home down (`:155-163`) | 1; ≈30 queries total (`getAvailability`/`getGoalView`/`listRequests`/`getLearningOverview` each called 2–3× but `cache()`d) |
| `child/learn/page.tsx` | 0 (dependent) | n/a (one service, which does `Promise.all` of 6 inside `learning/service.ts:47-54`) | 1 | 1 |
| `child/diary/page.tsx` | 0 | Yes (`:19`) | 1 | 1; `getHistoryPage` has a dependent 2nd trip (`ledger/service.ts:154-157`) |
| `child/requests/page.tsx` | 0 | Yes ×5 (`:17-23`) | 1 | 1 |
| `child/inbox/page.tsx` | 0 | Yes (`:16`) | 1 | 1 |
| `dashboard/page.tsx` | 0 | — | 1 | 1 |
| `parent/page.tsx` | 0 (`ChildCard` awaits `read.goal` after `Promise.all` at `:192-197` but the promise was started at `:63`) | Yes; all per-child reads started up front (`:58-66`) and settled per section with `allSettled` | 3 + N child cards; requests failing per child shows a calm line (`:170-189`) | 1; ≈50 queries for 3 children (#2) |
| `parent/children/[childId]/page.tsx` | **2** — `ChildGuide` awaits `getGuideAccess` then `countFlaggedChats` (`:139-141`); both only need `childId` | Yes (`:35-40`) | 5 (outer, requests, learning, guide, history) | 1 |
| `parent/children/[childId]/history/page.tsx` | **1** — `loadChild` then `getHistoryPage` (`:18-19`); `getHistoryPage` only needs `childId` and does its own authz (`ledger/service.ts:143`) | No | 1 | 1 |
| `parent/allowance/page.tsx` | 0 | Yes, nested per child (`:16, 20-25`) | 1 | 1 |
| `parent/inbox/page.tsx` | 0 | Yes (`:16`) | 1 | 1 |

One slow query blocking a page: on the child Home only `requireRole` + the four top-level reads (nickname, family, schedule, due) block the h1; every other section streams. On `parent/children/[childId]` the jars/due/schedule/gifts block the shell (`:35-40`), the rest streams. All outer boundaries use `SlowFallback` (`src/components/shell/slow-fallback.tsx`) which offers retry at 10 s and reloads 5 s after.

## C. Offline outbox — assessment summary

- **Durability**: IndexedDB via `idb`, store-before-send (`submit.ts:54-57`). No IndexedDB → `open()` returns `null` (`store.ts:66-96`) and the item is sent directly with the same key; offline + no storage → `unsaved` (`submit.ts:23-29`), surfaced to the child rather than lost silently.
- **Ordering**: oldest first by `createdAt` then `id` (`store.ts:48-52`), `createdNow()` is strictly increasing within a tab (`submit.ts:7-13`); drain stops at the first `retry`/`unauthorized` so the head holds the queue (`flush.ts:42-62`). Undo is not an outbox kind, so no purchase→undo dependency can reorder.
- **Duplicates**: `id = idempotencyKey` for purchases (`submit.ts:44`); one drainer per user per tab (`running` set) and across tabs via `navigator.locks.request(..., {ifAvailable: true})` (`flush.ts:19-31`); a flush requested while busy is queued once (`again`, `:88-103`). There is **no persisted `sending` state** (the type's `"sending"` at `types.ts:21` is never written), so nothing can get stuck after a crash — correct choice.
- **Size bounds**: none on entry count; 7-day age (`state.ts:9`); 500 entries would just drain sequentially at one request each. Fine.
- **Retry policy**: backoff 2/5/15/30 s then 60 s cap, 20 attempts; 408/429/5xx/network → retry, 401 → hold, other 4xx → refused (purchases to "needs a look", learning items dropped with a log, `state.ts:16-22`). **See finding #1** for the 20-attempt budget.
- **Sign-out / switching child**: keyed by `userId` end to end; server enforces `body.userId === session user` (`route.ts:63-65`) and re-runs each feature's own Zod schema (`schema.ts:15-28`) and `assertRole("child")` inside the service. Server derives `occurredAt` from `ageMs`, clamped (`route.ts:21-30`). One entry per request (not batched); per-entry result is the normal `{data}` / `{error}` envelope.

## D. Realtime and push — assessment summary

- Unique channel topic per mount (`realtime.ts:44`), `removeChannel` in the cleanup (`:62-65`), `setAuth()` before subscribe (`:56-58`), effect deps `[userId, viewer, currency, router]` (`notice-listener.tsx:52`). StrictMode double-mount is explicitly handled by the random topic suffix. Reconnect: `onStatus(true)` on every `SUBSCRIBED` → `router.refresh()` (`:43-46`), so events missed during a drop are picked up.
- Push: claim with `for update skip locked` and a 200-row cap (`push_subscriptions.sql:125-142`), batch of 50 after actions / 200 in the sweep (`push.ts:26, 75`), 8 in flight (`:22`), 10 s per send (`:24`), `TTL` 1 h, 404/410 → row deleted (`:107-111`), one endpoint failing never aborts the batch (`runLimited`, `:29-38`), 15 s drain budget inside `maxDuration = 30` (`cron/push/route.ts:7-9`). Daily cron is within Vercel Hobby limits. **See finding #3** for cron-generated notices and 429.
- Payload: `title`, `body` (the inbox line, which can include an amount and the child's nickname), same-origin `url`, `tag` (`push.ts:56-64`); encrypted end to end by Web Push, shown on the lock screen — a product decision, not a leak. `public/sw.js` caches nothing and forces same-origin on click (`:25-34`).

## E. Guide — assessment summary

- Timeouts: 20 s per Anthropic call with `maxRetries: 1` (`claude.ts:33, 124`), 45 s turn deadline via `AbortSignal.timeout` + `AbortSignal.any` shared by all calls (`pipeline.ts:147, 196-198`), route `maxDuration = 60` (`turns/route.ts:6`), client `TURN_TIMEOUT_MS = 52_000` (`guide-sheet.tsx:40`). Tool look-ups rely on the 8 s Supabase fetch deadline (#7).
- Breaker: 5 failures / 60 s window / 60 s open, in-memory per instance (`breaker.ts:8-16, 36`). Per-instance on serverless is weak but documented as the "cheap version"; the hard cost cap is DB-backed (`budgetSpent`, `service.ts:67-80`, aggregate RPC `guide_usage_since`).
- Streaming: NDJSON status lines then one `reply` or a terminal `error` event (`stream.ts:21-51`); nothing partial reaches the child; if the stream ends with no reply the client throws and `runOnline` shows the "slow" copy. Turn is stored even if the client went away (`stream.ts:14-16`), so a re-send with the same `clientMessageId` replays (`service.ts:104-133, 177-178`; unique index `retry_safe_guide_turn.sql:18`).
- Tool loop bounded: `MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS = 3` (`provider.ts:70`; `claude.ts:201-203, 218-220`).
- Cost: usage summed across rounds (`claude.ts:166, 187-191`), carried on errors (`withUsage`, `claude.ts:229`; `pipeline.ts:236, 245, 253`), cache read/write counted; a retried call cannot double-count because usage is read from the one successful response. Budget check is before the call and non-atomic → N concurrent turns can overshoot by N × one turn (cents); acceptable. Prices match (#12).
- Retries: SDK retries 408/409/429/5xx once with its own jittered backoff; no app-level retry loop. Fallback: `fake-provider.ts` is refused unless `GUIDE_ALLOW_FAKE` and `VERCEL_ENV !== "production"` (`config.ts:15-19`) — never in prod.

## F. Misc

- `next.config.ts:12-14`: `cacheComponents: true`, `partialPrefetching: true`, `typedRoutes`; security headers; no `images` config (no remote images). Segment configs: only `maxDuration` on four learning pages (60 s, for the in-page guide action), the guide route (60), outbox (30), cron (30). No `dynamic`/`revalidate`/`runtime` overrides.
- No `"use cache"`, `cacheLife` or `cacheTag` anywhere (grep) → no shared-cache leak surface. Every mutating action calls `refresh()` (allowance/requests/rules/diary/profiles/family/guide) or `revalidatePath` (#10), and the client sheets call `router.refresh()` on success.
- Health: `/api/health` liveness; `?deep=1` runs one anon `head` count on `families` and returns 503 `degraded` on failure (`health/route.ts:9-17`, `lib/supabase/health.ts:9-20`), bounded by the 8 s `supabaseFetch`.

---

## Done well (evidence)

- Session read once per request, no GoTrue round trip: `cache()` + `getClaims()` (`session.ts:15-20`); `requireUser`/`assertUser` are thin wrappers (`:37-48`).
- Family context, role and parent-of-child checks are per-request memoised (`dal.ts:17, 24, 80-82`), with the comment explaining the per-child cache purpose.
- Hot read services are `cache()`d so repeated calls across islands dedupe: `getAvailability`, `listRequests` (`requests/service.ts:49, 64`), `getGoalView` (`goals/service.ts:17`), `getLearningOverview` (`learning/service.ts:42`), `getWeeklyReview` (`review/service.ts:30`).
- Every Supabase client (server, admin, proxy) uses the 8 s `createTimedFetch` with slow-call logging (`timed-fetch.ts:40-87`; `server.ts:20`, `admin.ts:23`, `proxy.ts:22`); timeouts become a JA503 → `UNAVAILABLE`, never a sign-out (`session.ts:23-27`).
- Pages: `Promise.all` everywhere; parent Home starts all per-child reads before rendering and settles per section with `Promise.allSettled` (`parent/page.tsx:58-72, 140-144, 171`); fail-soft islands with `unstable_rethrow` + `logger.warn` (`child/page.tsx:116-121, 155-163`).
- Money writes are single Postgres functions with client idempotency keys (`requests/repository.ts:153-162`; `create_withdrawal` etc.), and the outbox reuses the same keys.
- Guide: DB-backed per-minute/day limits (`service.ts:36-38, 213-218`), aggregate budget query (`guide.sql:285-318`), bounded tool rounds, deadline shared via one `AbortSignal`, replies never streamed unscreened, token usage preserved through failures, fake provider blocked in production.
- Outbox: Web Locks + in-tab guard, oldest-first with head-of-line hold, store-before-send, server-side re-validation with the feature schemas and a `userId` match, device-clock-proof `occurredAt`.
- Realtime: unique topic per mount, `removeChannel` on unmount, refresh on every (re)join so no event is missed across a disconnect.
- Push: `for update skip locked` claim, bounded batches/concurrency/timeouts, dead-endpoint cleanup, `after()` so forms never wait on a push service; service worker caches nothing and forces same-origin navigation.
- Lists are paginated with keyset cursors (inbox) or time cursors (ledger), and there is no `select("*")` on wide tables.
- Health probe answers 503 on database failure for the uptime monitor.
